If they did, then the three beings looking at the glass of water would have to think of the water as a place to live, in the way that a creature living in water would. The three beings as well would have to see the water in the same way that microscopic organisms living in the water, little beings imperceptible to normal visual consciousness, see it with their own visual consciousness. Then too the visual consciousness of microscopic organisms living in the depths of the ocean would have to be a valid perception towards the entire extent of the sea. And certain kinds of near-gods too would have to see weapons as glasses of water, and on and on; the problems raised would be many."
  }
]

===== root-member-group-clarification.md =====
# Independent review challenge: contiguous compound members

Root found a material disagreement after the initial304–306 intake. Reconciler omitted the original English306 rnag/pus member solely to keep one immediately-following child under rnag khrag. It otherwise calls rnag/pus semantically sound.

The immutable canonical gen_alignment_page.py with_members function (lines113–147) explicitly supports multiple consecutive depth7 children of the same depth5 parent, including crossed English member order, and cites C03:207 fourth/state. Its resolve function retains the parent range across contiguous depth7 spans. The condensed spec says a member must immediately follow its parent; root interprets this as the contiguous group below the last depth5, consistent with canonical explicit semantics, not a one-child limit. Each child must still be unique and strictly tighter in both languages. Canonical passing alone does not prove semantic correctness.

Fresh reviewer must independently resolve this interpretation from the actual governing code/spec/brief and useful defensible coverage. Do not inherit the one-child cap without evidence. Record the disagreement even if omission is retained. No current spec/body/source or accepted data changed by this note; minimal correction remains root-owned after an independent verdict. The earlier intake remains a truthful earlier boundary, not final approval.

Exact origin ce646d0c48f9338ff47e40f46582ad14c8a3eaf4; canonical bytes 21175, SHA256 7c1c22345e18949e1aa34bb99115c32903527643b5a2c171e1a88f06d2a4cb8e.

===== tibetan/304/report.md =====
C05:304 · independent Tibetan-first · Codex (GPT-6 Astra) · PROVISIONAL, unreviewed.

Original source origin ce646d0c48f9338ff47e40f46582ad14c8a3eaf4 through294; future through303 acceptance is not asserted.

Challenge first the d3 body-washing recast lus 'khrud pa → washes himself; it preserves the whole verb-object construction while avoiding a false lus → himself dictionary entry.

The original master has blos rlom pa → imagination (glossary tier), dngos gnas pa → real, and 'khrud pa → washes. C15:449 matches all three ACIP/Wylie/English fields; P7:43 ends at his and supports only the shared extent.

Second mi/de and closing zer na are distributed in the English reference/claim frame and omitted. with the water is supplied. yin receives an explicit contraction-fusion null; null does not mean absent meaning.

No new erratum: physical lines 3025–3029 reproduce the original ACIP and, after whitespace joining, the complete English. No cap/heading class occurs. Exact C15 field identity establishes wording only, not independent publication or lineage.

Counts: 15 spans; 11 nonnull; 4 null; 8 d5 (7 nonnull); 0 errata. Automatically generated d1 source envelopes are excluded from these counts.

Canonical result: EXIT=0; stderr bytes=0; body bytes=3226. body.html equals ../native/gen-304-v1.stdout byte for byte; input/command/stderr/exit are beside that file.

Hashes: spec.json SHA-256 8138e23525cdbeb656ec49c3805cf6703be100fa1ddd62ea0ebc6a6794e6b412; body.html SHA-256 bc24d858e2a03723a8e333834a782a4c4edfe01a8e5c6f59f90b373ea8831bb2.

Range proof: mechanical-proof.json; intended-ranges.json holds exact occurrence choices, ownership, depth and omissions. Full text reconstruction and historical builder parsing pass; these checks do not certify semantics.

Shared original evidence: ../evidence/evidence-index.json SHA-256 f1b35397a9885ae375e20d0db2e96f97159e67f68dc9aade591826c6e47755c4; per-span claims map through ../evidence/span-evidence-map.json; five errata grounds are in errata-review.json.

Landing allowance proposed: c5p102/s304w4 licensed by sogs. No repository allowance was modified.

Attempts: canonical generation passed on v1. Both preserved helper failures (collect-evidence-v1 decoding and write-reports-v1 quoting) have exact native errors and failed code in ../native/ and ../helpers/; v2 helpers succeeded. No canonical spec failed. Final freeze is ../freeze-manifest.json.

===== english/304/report.md =====
C05:304 — English-first independent proposal by Codex; PROVISIONAL, no semantic approval.
The d3 `lus 'khrud pa` → “washes himself” is the first pairing to challenge: it captures a complete body-washing recast, not lus → himself. Contextual “with the water” stays outside spans.
`blos rlom pa` → “imagination” is an established HGM expression. The second mi/possessive and closing speech/copular frame are distributed, omitted with reasons; no nulls disguise that uncertainty.

Proposal/governance origin: ce646d0c48f9338ff47e40f46582ad14c8a3eaf4 through294. Future through303 acceptance is distinct and not asserted.
The shared d3 body-washing recast is retained as a complete relation; neither lus/himself nor supplied water is banked separately.
Whole blos rlom pa/imagination has positive HGM24295 glossary evidence. tsam owns nothing more than; med/not is d5 and the full consequence stays negative.
The four grammatical nulls have explicit fusion reasons; second mi/de and zer na are omitted as distributed. sogs stays d5 with its named and licensor.
Original dispositions: 26; every original ID, span object, reason/decision source and actual range preserved in original-span-dispositions.json.

===== tibetan/305/report.md =====
C05:305 · independent Tibetan-first · Codex (GPT-6 Astra) · PROVISIONAL, unreviewed.

Original source origin ce646d0c48f9338ff47e40f46582ad14c8a3eaf4 through294; future through303 acceptance is not asserted.

Challenge first yang → too across the sentence boundary: the additive comparison survives in For that too. First thal selects first then; final thal selects must.

The original master has bsreg pa → sizzle, lto → stomach, blos rlom pa → imagination, and curated sdug bsngal → suffering. C15:450 matches ACIP/Wylie/English exactly; P7:44 preserves the English with differing Tibetan punctuation and DWAGS spelling.

Second yi dvags is pronominalized and omitted; source occurrence 3 owns final craving spirits. la/na are recast and omitted. The supplied pus and blood, modal can, auxiliary be, and emphatic at all remain unwrapped.

No new erratum: physical lines 3031–3035 confirm the selected wording. bsreg pa/sizzle is supported by the original HGM glossary. Registered-class and corpus checks give no source correction to file.

Counts: 24 spans; 20 nonnull; 4 null; 16 d5 (16 nonnull); 0 errata. Automatically generated d1 source envelopes are excluded from these counts.

Canonical result: EXIT=0; stderr bytes=0; body bytes=4177. body.html equals ../native/gen-305-v1.stdout byte for byte; input/command/stderr/exit are beside that file.

Hashes: spec.json SHA-256 5d54de37a146234c6b1e61e0425c17cca14f81172ef8d0f4e9b11132c86612f6; body.html SHA-256 6fe733cbad34cb5cdbfe79281de4b67d168300e65c2d8d8e1d62a1ccce9e2e3a.

Range proof: mechanical-proof.json; intended-ranges.json holds exact occurrence choices, ownership, depth and omissions. Full text reconstruction and historical builder parsing pass; these checks do not certify semantics.

Shared original evidence: ../evidence/evidence-index.json SHA-256 f1b35397a9885ae375e20d0db2e96f97159e67f68dc9aade591826c6e47755c4; per-span claims map through ../evidence/span-evidence-map.json; five errata grounds are in errata-review.json.

Landing allowance proposed: c5p102/s305w5 licensed by sogs. No repository allowance was modified.

Attempts: canonical generation passed on v1. Both preserved helper failures (collect-evidence-v1 decoding and write-reports-v1 quoting) have exact native errors and failed code in ../native/ and ../helpers/; v2 helpers succeeded. No canonical spec failed. Final freeze is ../freeze-manifest.json.

===== english/305/report.md =====
C05:305 — English-first independent proposal by Codex; PROVISIONAL, no semantic approval.
The displaced `yang` → “too” is the first pairing to challenge; the English puts it after For that. Exact eng_order and independently intended ranges locate this later occurrence.
The first/third yi dvags map to singular/plural craving spirit(s); the middle occurrence is pronominal and omitted. med maps to n't of can't, then to not exist. Supplied pus and blood stays unwrapped.
Original HGM entries support bsreg pa → sizzle, lto → stomach and blos rlom pa → imagination. C15:450 matches all three text fields; P7:44 matches English while ACIP/Wylie differ.
Errata: [] after full original comparisons and registered-class review. First thal, conditional na and copular embedding remain unbanked as distributed rhetoric.

===== reconciled/305/report.md =====
C05:305 — independently reconciled by Codex (GPT-6 Astra); PROVISIONAL, not human approved.
Proposal/governance origin: ce646d0c48f9338ff47e40f46582ad14c8a3eaf4 through294. Future through303 acceptance is distinct and not asserted.
Retain displaced yang/too at E[141:144], with separate For/phyir, restrictive tsam and exact yin/is. Supplied pus and blood remain unwrapped.
Remove first par/thal: the consequence is spread across the opening question and modal frame, so first then is not banked at d5. The final thal/must remains.
Keep first/third yi dvags as singular/plural spirit, middle occurrence omitted; both med negations and curated suffering remain. Three nulls have explicit fusion reasons.
Original dispositions: 42; every original ID, span object, reason/decision source and actual range preserved in original-span-dispositions.json.

===== tibetan/306/report.md =====
C05:306 · independent Tibetan-first · Codex (GPT-6 Astra) · PROVISIONAL, unreviewed.

Original source origin ce646d0c48f9338ff47e40f46582ad14c8a3eaf4 through294; future through303 acceptance is not asserted.

Challenge first the d3 temporal/first-conjunct constructions pa'i tshe, pa de'i tshe, and gang zhig; they encode local grammar, while lexical look/looks stay d5.

Master glossary explicitly supports bse ru → porcelain basin/basin, rab rib can → person with cataracts, skra shad → strand of hair, dbang po → sense power, and mtshungs pa → identical to. C15:451 matches three text fields; P7:45 has the same English with Tibetan differences. ILL:2323 preserves a differing verse with [vi.71a] and a different English rendering.

med selects n't in the second isn't, after look; the initial rhetorical Isn't is unwrapped. de ltar/par thal, possessor can, flowing 'bab, and zer na remain omitted as distributed/unresolved. Bracketed author, sit down, and expanded participants remain verbatim and unwrapped.

No new erratum: physical lines 3037–3049 confirm all wording. The bracketed [Master Dharmakirti's] attribution remains an unresolved source attribution, not an aligned claim. ILL:2319–2326 supplies contextual verse evidence but does not by itself establish a correction or independent publication. No authorship correction is filed.

Counts: 37 spans; 31 nonnull; 6 null; 21 d5 (21 nonnull); 0 errata. Automatically generated d1 source envelopes are excluded from these counts.

Canonical result: EXIT=0; stderr bytes=0; body bytes=6466. body.html equals ../native/gen-306-v1.stdout byte for byte; input/command/stderr/exit are beside that file.

Hashes: spec.json SHA-256 ad0a26026ea2d5ffbf04ae9d6fb920f446863d90073529ee47ff7a545084f85a; body.html SHA-256 365399482d15a291d262283afbbb5f627bb8153ace8314871ff31e77dfde4128.

Range proof: mechanical-proof.json; intended-ranges.json holds exact occurrence choices, ownership, depth and omissions. Full text reconstruction and historical builder parsing pass; these checks do not certify semantics.

Shared original evidence: ../evidence/evidence-index.json SHA-256 f1b35397a9885ae375e20d0db2e96f97159e67f68dc9aade591826c6e47755c4; per-span claims map through ../evidence/span-evidence-map.json; five errata grounds are in errata-review.json.

No article/hinge head allowance is requested for this segment.

Attempts: canonical generation passed on v1. Both preserved helper failures (collect-evidence-v1 decoding and write-reports-v1 quoting) have exact native errors and failed code in ../native/ and ../helpers/; v2 helpers succeeded. No canonical spec failed. Final freeze is ../freeze-manifest.json.

===== english/306/report.md =====
C05:306 — English-first independent proposal by Codex; PROVISIONAL, no semantic approval.
Challenge the d3 `cig car du` → “sit down together” recast and temporal tshe → when first; life in the master tshe entry is another sense, not the evidence for this temporal use.
Repeated when, basin and pus have separate intended offsets, checked against actual HTML. Four nulls mark only non-separate case/quotation exponents; possessor can, flowing 'bab and premise framing remain omitted with specific reasons.
Original HGM evidence supports bse ru and skra shad compounds. C15:451 matches all three text fields; P7:45 matches English with Tibetan differences; full ILL:2320–2328 was reopened for the variant quoted verse.
Errata: one PROBABLE English attribution candidate quotes [Master Dharmakirti's] unchanged. C05:247 supports only its shared second line; ILL:2323 supplies both variant lines. No independent bibliographic confirmation or publication/ingestion lineage is claimed.

===== reconciled/306/report.md =====
C05:306 — independently reconciled by Codex (GPT-6 Astra); PROVISIONAL, not human approved.
Proposal/governance origin: ce646d0c48f9338ff47e40f46582ad14c8a3eaf4 through294. Future through303 acceptance is distinct and not asserted.
Reject cig car du/sit down together despite its glossary recast; together is the supported d5 atom. Full temporal and locative constructions stay d3.
Retain negative existential med/isn't at E[128:133], distinct from rhetorical Isn't; tighten second med pa to med/no. Entire negative propositions are reviewed explicitly.
HGM21422 and HGM889 support the two basin and hair senses. Retain skra/hair member; both unique tighter d7 children rnag/pus (mPus) and khrag/blood (w6) are kept contiguously under rnag khrag (w5).
Use established dang ldan/has at d5 for the cataract-bearing sense power. External can stays omitted; gang zhig/first of all marks the first premise.
Original attribution candidate was MEDIUM/PROBABLE. After all five grounds, no erratum is retained: internal witnesses motivate bibliographic checking but do not establish the correction. Concern remains LOW/UNCERTAIN in evidence.
Original dispositions: 73; every original ID, span object, reason/decision source and actual range preserved in original-span-dispositions.json.
Final counts: 38 spans; 33 nonnull; 5 null; 21 d5; 5 d7; 0 errata. Generated source envelopes are excluded.

{
  "tibetan": "PROVISIONAL machine alignment by Codex (GPT-6 Astra), independent Tibetan-first proposal; not human approved and no hgm_gloss promotion. The med span selects n't in the second isn't, after look; the initial rhetorical Isn't is unwrapped. Sit down, explanatory participants, and [Master Dharmakirti's] text itself remain verbatim but unwrapped. The exact C15:451 parallel and differing ILL:2323 verse do not establish independent publication or settle authorship. Unresolved de ltar, thal, can, 'bab and closing zer na remain omitted with reasons in the occurrence ledger.",
  "english": "PROVISIONAL machine alignment by Codex (English-first independent proposal); GMR English is verbatim. Mechanical generation does not establish semantic approval. \"sit down together\" is a d3 recast of simultaneous viewing; it does not add a dictionary equation for literal sitting. Repeated look/looks, when/when, basin/basin and pus/pus are separately located. skra shad owns \"strand of hair,\" with only skra as a tighter \"hair\" member. The genitive suffix of bse ru'i and locative du have no separate exponents beyond the spatial construction \"in the basin\"; gyis has no separate word in the active subject. The premise enumeration, de ltar, dbang po can possessor framing and the flowing modifier 'bab are distributed and remain unbanked. The bracketed attribution [Master Dharmakirti's] remains verbatim, unwrapped apparatus; an internal attribution inconsistency is proposed as PROBABLE in errata.json, with no source correction. The next-row reply (C05:307) distinguishes perception validity from the actual presence of pus and blood."
